NLA vs J-lens / J++ (exp16_nla)

  • Cards: the 77 two-hop questions Qwen3.6-27B answers correctly.
  • Model's answer: the first line of the model's own greedy continuation of the prompt (exp14), and the same with the bridge swapped to its distractor on the cue phrase (exp14's J++ swap); "unchanged" = still the right answer; "changed to the swap target's answer" = e.g. Spanish after Brazil -> Mexico; "changed, but to neither answer" = something else (e.g. 11 instead of 5 or 9).
  • Rows: where the NLA reads (the last word, -1, the cue phrase's last token) and at which layer. Filter the layers with the second row of buttons.
  • Rank columns: where the J-lens and J++ rank the bridge among the 97 entities, and the answer among the 112 answers, at that position and layer (exp12); 1 = first. The two lists are separate, so the bridge and the answer can both be 1st in one row.
  • Highlighted rows: neither lens ranks the bridge 1st there; those rows put a question in its group.
  • NLA columns: the NLA's words for the same activation (greedy, 160 tokens), before and after exp14's J++ bridge swap on the cue phrase. Marked: bridge, answer, their distractors (numeric answers are not marked: list numbering).
  • Data: runs results/exp16_nla/20261015-144540; regenerate with uv run python -m scripts.exp15_oracle_lens.examples <run> [<run> ...].
